# Stage E.12 Task 2c: EC-K9 announcement dates, 2019-05-01 to 2024-02-29

Built by CalendarBuilder-OpusHigh from official Federal Reserve Board, BLS, BEA and ISM pages (ISM through Wayback Machine captures), saved raw under reports/stage_e12_briefs/calendar_pages/. Machine-readable file: reports/stage_e12_ec_k9_2019_2024.json (schema ec_k9/1). Fetch log: reports/stage_e12_briefs/calendarbuilder_log.md. No released value is recorded.

## Rule (C9)

> The announcements are the FOMC, Non-farm payroll, GDP (first and last), ISM manufacturing PMI, and the earlier of the CPI and PPI announcements. (C9 / K9-anncday-01, P-K9C-003-c); inflation: "We combine the PPI and CPI announcements into one “inflation” announcement by using the earlier one each month" (P-K9C-003-d); mapping (catalog round 2 item 11): FOMC = statement day of each scheduled meeting; NFP = Employment Situation release day; GDP first = advance estimate, last = third estimate; inflation = the earlier of CPI and PPI per reference month; ISM dates are scheduled dates. Dates known in advance only (known before the 17:59 CT entry intent on the evening before d).

Times of day are not recorded; FOMC dates are statement days, all other dates are release days.

## Questions for the lead (cases kept out of date_set or open)

### Q1: Unscheduled FOMC meetings, notation votes and press releases in the window (FOMC)

The Fed historical pages label these "(unscheduled)" or "(notation vote)". None is among the meetings on the Fed calendars captured 2018-12-20 (2019 schedule) and 2019-12-20 (2020 schedule), which list the scheduled meetings only. No source found shows any of them announced before the evening before (writer's reading; the lead rules). 2020-03-15 is a Sunday (no trade date d of its own). 2020-08-27 is the Statement on Longer-Run Goals and Monetary Policy Strategy (the E.10 analogue 2025-08-22 was ruled not a meeting, CAL-FED-3).

- {"entry": "October 4 (unscheduled) - 2019", "release_date": "2019-10-11", "weekday": "Friday", "sources": ["CAL-E12-876"]}
- {"entry": "March 2 (unscheduled) Meeting - 2020", "release_date": "2020-03-03", "weekday": "Tuesday", "sources": ["CAL-E12-877"]}

### Q2: Scheduled March 17-18, 2020 meeting cancelled (FOMC)

Listed on the 2020 historical page as "(cancelled)". It was on the pre-announced schedule (Fed calendar captured 2019-12-20, cited in the sources of this case), but no statement was released on 2020-03-18. The unscheduled Sunday 2020-03-15 meeting and statement came before the 17:59 CT entry intent on 2020-03-17. The historical page does not itself say when the cancellation was announced.

- {"entry": "March 17-18 (cancelled) Meeting - 2020", "scheduled_statement_day": "2020-03-18", "sources": ["CAL-E12-879", "CAL-E12-884"]}

Status: kept out of date_set (no statement day); lead to rule

### Q3: Scheduled versus actual ISM release days (ISM_manufacturing_scheduled)

May 2019 - Jul 2020: each scheduled date is also confirmed as the actual release by the "FOR RELEASE:" line of the next capture of the manufacturing report page. Aug 2020 - Feb 2024: scheduled dates from ISM calendar captures only; that each release occurred on its scheduled day is [unverified] (same status as E.10 CAL-ISM rows). 2020 calendar captures (20200809, 20201028) carry no year heading; the year is taken from the capture date, corroborated by the 20210117 capture ("December 2020 ... 1") and by the 2020 FOR RELEASE lines for Jan-Jul.

Status: informational
